<li class="Z1qcYe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIORAB" data-complete="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><span class="iNqyIf" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Slow Response Latency (The Golden Rule):<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> If a sick patient messages the clinic on WhatsApp and the front desk responds 4 hours later, the conversion probability drops to near zero. Leads responded to <strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">within 3 minutes<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> convert over 2 times more effectively than those left waiting.<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></span> [<a href="https://ichelonconsulting.com/insights/lead-to-appointment-conversion-rate-benchmark">1</a>]<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></li>
<li class="Z1qcYe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIORAG" data-complete="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><span class="iNqyIf" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Consultation Fee Friction:<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> If your automated greeting script instantly demands payment in the very first message (<em class="zhvwWe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">"Pay ₹300 first to talk to us"<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></em>), users will abandon the chat. The price conversation should always happen in message #3 or after the representative understands the primary medical concern.<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></span> [<a href="https://ichelonconsulting.com/insights/lead-to-appointment-conversion-rate-benchmark">1</a>]<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></li>
<li class="Z1qcYe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-hveid="CAAIORAL" data-complete="true" data-sae="" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px 0px 12px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><span class="iNqyIf" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);"><strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Targeting General Queries instead of Specific Pain Points:<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong> Running a general ad saying <em class="zhvwWe" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 400; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">"Consult a top doctor online"<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></em> yields weak, unqualified interest. Running targeted ads matching the site's unique offerings&mdash;such as <strong class="rQesXe MPyX" data-sfc-cp="" data-sfc-root="ep" data-complete="true" data-copy-service-computed-style="font-family: &quot;Google Sans&quot;, Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 700; margin: 0px; text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 0px rgb(10, 10, 10);">Uterine Fibroids Management or Chronic Sciatica Pain Relief<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></strong>&mdash;attracts individuals ready to take action immediately.<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></span> [<a href="https://redwudcreations.com/2026/01/10/google-ads-for-hospitals-complete-cost-roi-campaign-strategy-guide-for-india/">1</a>, <a href="https://digifinity.in/meta-ads-vs-google-ads-indian-businesses/">2</a>]<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></li>
<!--TgQPHd|||[]--></ol>
